Every URL cited above is therefore a LIVE, verified link; no archive or fallback URL is needed for any of them, and none has been removed.
EVERY EDITION IS DATED FROM ITS OWN COVER PAGE, NOT FROM ITS FILENAME AND NOT FROM THE INDEX LABEL, because for this Bank the three disagree. Two confirmed disagreements: (1) `basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf` - the FILENAME IS WRONG. Its cover reads 'Basel II - Pillar 3 disclosures for the year ended March 31, 2012', and the index label agrees ('for 2011- 2012'); only the filename says 2011. The genuine FY2011 edition is the separate file `basel2_disclosures_FY10_11.pdf`, whose cover reads 'year ended 31 March 2011'. This workbook maps them accordingly. (2) `disclosures2013-14.pdf` - the INDEX LABEL IS WRONG ON REGIME. The index calls it 'Basel II Pillar 3 Disclosures for 2013-2014', but the document's own cover and every page's running head read 'Basel III - Pillar 3 Disclosures / 31 March 2014', and its section 2 describes implementing CRD IV from January 1, 2014. The period (year ended 31 March 2014) is right; the regime label is not. FY2014 is this Bank's FIRST CRD IV year and is treated as such throughout this workbook - it is NOT grouped with the Basel II years. Every other edition's filename, index label and cover agree with one another.
ENTITY RE-CONFIRMED ON ALL 19: each opens "ICICI Bank UK PLC ('the Bank') ... a wholly owned subsidiary of ICICI Bank Limited" and states the disclosures are prepared for ICICI Bank UK PLC, expressly distinguishing itself from the parent's own consolidated disclosures. No ICICI Bank Limited (parent) figure enters any sheet. One hosting-path artefact worth recording so it is not mistaken for a parent-entity document: the FY2024 edition is served from an `/content/dam/icicibank/india/managed-assets/...` path, but the document itself is the UK entity's, verified on its own cover and Overview section.
PRINTED-TOTAL SANITY CHECK ON EVERY DISCLOSED RWA. For each of FY2017-FY2026, that year's own CET1 and total capital divided by that year's own printed total RWA reproduce that year's own printed CET1 and total capital ratios, confirming the printed total is a genuine total and not a credit-risk subtotal mislabelled as one. The single departure is FY2024 (311.3 / 1,546.2 = 20.13% against a printed 20.14%), which is rounding in the one-decimal-place capital figure, not a basis difference. For FY2021-FY2026 the printed total additionally equals the exact sum of its own printed UK OV1 components.
SECOND, NON-PDF SOURCE CHECKED (2026-09-16) - 'Composition of Regulatory Capital' web disclosures. The same Basel Disclosures index also links ten HTML pages (not PDFs, so they are easily missed when enumerating the index's PDF links), each titled 'Disclosure: Composition of Regulatory Capital as at March 31, YYYY' and presented in USD'000. All ten were fetched on 2026-09-16 (HTTP 200, text/html) and read. They corroborate this workbook's capital figures from an independent source: FY2014 Total Tier one capital after deductions 495,095 + 89,759 + 50,000 - 11,061 = 623,794 (workbook 623.8) and Tier one plus tier two after deductions 814,419 (workbook 814.4); FY2015 538,819 (538.8) and 707,047 (707.0); FY2016 537,633 (537.6) and 684,316 (684.3). They contain NO risk-weighted assets, NO risk-weighted exposure amount and NO capital ratio of any kind, so they do not and cannot supply the FY2014-FY2016 total RWA. Note the difference in vintage terminology: these pages use the pre-CRD IV 'Core Tier one / Upper tier two / Lower tier two' captions even for FY2014-FY2016, whereas the corresponding Pillar 3 editions use CRD IV captions for the same amounts; no figure has been mapped across that caption difference beyond the totals above, which are identical either way. URLs: FY2019 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-capital-resource-popup-march2019; FY2018 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-capital-resource-popup-march2018; FY2017 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-capital-resource-popup-march2017; FY2016 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-capital-resource-popup-march2016; FY2015 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2015; FY2014 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2014; FY2013 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2013; FY2012 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2012; FY2011 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2011; FY2010 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup.
FY2008-FY2013 - BASEL II ERA, KEY METRICS NOT DISCLOSED (enumerated, not merely unfound). The Bank published an annual Pillar 3 disclosure for every one of these years and all six were downloaded, cover-date-verified and read in full on 2026-09-15. They are short (10 pages each) BIPRU Chapter 11 / Basel II documents, a different regulatory regime from the CRR / Basel III disclosures used for FY2014 onward, and they contain NO capital resources table, NO Tier 1 / Tier 2 / total capital figure, NO capital ratio, NO total risk-weighted assets, NO leverage ratio, NO LCR and NO NSFR. Their only capital content is a table captioned 'the Bank's Pillar 1 capital requirement by each of the standardised credit risk exposure classes' - credit risk ONLY, with no operational-risk or market-risk capital requirement anywhere in the document. Those printed credit-risk Pillar 1 capital requirement totals are, in USD million: FY2013 241.69, FY2012 260.31, FY2011 402.24, FY2010 459.49, FY2009 426.62, FY2008 335.02. They are recorded here for traceability and are deliberately NOT converted to an RWA figure. Two independent reasons: this workbook does not compute figures at all (as of 2026-09-16 the FY2014-FY2016 Total RWAs, which had been derived that way, were withdrawn - see the Total RWAs sheet's note); and even if it did, these documents disclose no operational-risk and no market-risk capital requirement, so a credit-risk-only conversion would silently understate RWA by the two missing components. No key-metric cell is therefore filled for FY2008-FY2013, and none can be from Pillar 3. Documents (dated from each cover page, NOT from the filename - `basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf` is the year ended March 31, 2012, not 2011): FY2013 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/disclosures2012-13.pdf; FY2012 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf; FY2011 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY10_11.pdf; FY2010 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY09_10.pdf; FY2009 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/Basel_II_p3_Disclosure_Statement.pdf; FY2008 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY2008.pdf. Entity confirmed on each: every one opens "ICICI Bank UK PLC ('the Bank') ... a wholly owned subsidiary of ICICI Bank Limited", states "The disclosures have been prepared for ICICI Bank UK PLC", and expressly distinguishes itself from "the consolidated Basel II - Pillar 3 Disclosures made by ICICI Bank Limited ('the Parent Bank')" - no parent-group figure is present in any of them.
ENTITY NOTE: ICICI Bank UK Plc (Companies House 04663024, FRN 223268, LEI 2138002XB6T14IGKGU43) is the UK-incorporated PRA-authorised bank entity. Companies House confirms it is an active public limited company, incorporated 11 February 2003, with accounts filed at Companies House through 31 March 2025; the FY2026 (year ended 31 March 2026) Annual Report and Pillar 3 disclosure are published on the Bank's own website and were used for FY2026, ahead of that year's Companies House filing. The official Basel disclosures identify the reporting entity as ICICI Bank UK PLC and present the UK KM1 data on a standalone Bank basis in USD millions. No parent-group figures have been substituted.
ICICI Bank UK Plc — RWA Breakdown
Bank (standalone), USD million. UK OV1 template - source: official Basel III Pillar 3 Disclosures. FY2014-FY2020 predate this Bank's adoption of the UK OV1 template: those years' Pillar 3 disclosures only break Pillar 1 minimum capital requirement down by risk type (Credit risk / Market risk / Operational risk), not RWA directly - the Credit risk, Market risk and Operational risk rows for FY2014-FY2020 are therefore computed as (that year's own disclosed capital requirement for the risk type) / 8% (CRR Article 92's fixed Pillar 1 minimum ratio), a mechanical unit conversion rather than an estimate; Counterparty credit risk, Securitisation and Amounts-below-thresholds are not broken out at all in those years' disclosures (folded into Credit risk) and are left blank rather than guessed. For FY2017-FY2020 the Total RWA row uses that year's own directly-disclosed 'Total risk-weighted assets' COREP figure, which does not sum exactly to the derived Credit/Market/Operational rows above it (gaps of under 0.6 USD million, i.e. rounding in the underlying capital-requirement figures) - shown explicitly rather than force-reconciled. FY2014-FY2016 HAVE NO TOTAL RWA ROW AT ALL (blank), CHANGED 2026-09-16. Those three cells previously carried 3,737.5 / 3,685.0 / 4,093.8, derived as that year's own printed 'Total Capital Resource requirement under Pillar 1' (FY2014 299.0, FY2015 294.8, FY2016 327.5 USD million) divided by 8%. All three editions were re-read in full on 2026-09-16 and disclose no total RWA anywhere, and no later edition carries a FY2014-FY2016 comparative, so the derived values have been withdrawn rather than left standing - this project transcribes figures, it does not compute them. See the Total RWAs sheet's own note for the full evidence and for the printed capital-requirement figures those editions DO give, which are preserved there. Note that the FY2014-FY2016 Credit/Market/Operational rows above are retained: they are the same documented mechanical (capital requirement / 8%) unit conversion applied uniformly across FY2014-FY2020 on this sheet, each anchored to a specific printed per-risk-type figure, and are captioned as such - unlike the Total row, they are not presented as a headline disclosed metric anywhere else in the workbook. For FY2021-FY2026, where this Bank uses the UK OV1 template directly, the printed Total RWA was verified on 2026-09-16 to equal the sum of its own printed credit / counterparty-credit / securitisation / market / operational risk components exactly; the 'Amounts below thresholds for deduction' row is a memo line already included within Credit risk and is not added again. See source note at bottom.

Note: BASIS BREAK AT FY2022 - the two pairs of rows above are NOT one series and must not be charted or compared as one. FY2022 onward is the UK KM1 template, rows 13 and 14, which the template itself captions 'Total exposure measure EXCLUDING claims on central banks' and 'Leverage ratio excluding claims on central banks'. FY2017-FY2021 is the earlier CRR leverage disclosure template (Table LRSum line 8 / Table LRCom line 21 'Total leverage ratio exposure', ratio at LRCom line 22), which is the FULL exposure measure with no central-bank exclusion - a materially larger denominator, so the earlier ratios are structurally lower than they would be on the later basis. The Bank marks the break itself: the FY2022 edition leaves the 31 March 2021 comparative blank in KM1 rows 13/14 and explains in its own note (h) that 'At March 31, 2021, the Bank's leverage ratio was 15.66% in accordance with the regulation applicable as at that date' (the FY2021 edition itself prints 15.7% - the same figure to its own rounding). FY2021's figures here are taken from the FY2021 edition's own CRR LRSum/LRCom tables, added 2026-09-15 when that edition was located; they were previously blank because the FY2022 KM1 does not carry them. The leverage ratio disclosure requirement did not yet apply to this Bank for FY2014-FY2016 and no figure appears in those years' Pillar 3 disclosures, so they are left blank rather than estimated; FY2008-FY2013's Basel II disclosures predate the leverage ratio concept entirely. Note also that the Bank states it is not itself in scope of the PRA's 3.25% minimum UK leverage requirement (that applies to LREQ firms with retail deposits of GBP 50bn or more) but manages to that level as a PRA expectation.
FY2014-FY2016 RE-VERIFIED AS AN ENUMERATED ABSENCE (2026-09-16): the FY2014, FY2015 and FY2016 editions were re-read in full and full-text searched for 'leverage'. The word does not appear at all in the FY2014, FY2015 or FY2016 edition - there is no leverage section, no LRSum/LRCom table and no ratio. This Bank's first leverage disclosure is the FY2017 edition's section 10, which opens 'At March 31, 2017, the Bank's leverage ratio was 13.97%'. The three blanks are therefore a confirmed absence, not an unsearched gap, and should not be re-chased.

FY2021 SOURCE UPGRADE (2026-09-15): FY2021 previously relied on the 31 March 2021 comparative column of the FY2022 edition. Its own FY2021 edition has since been located and read, and it reproduces every figure already carried here exactly - CET1 493.9, Tier 1 493.9, Total capital 586.7, Total RWA 2,075.1, CET1/Tier 1 ratio 23.80%, total capital ratio 28.27% (Annexure I transitional own funds template rows 59-63 and LRCom row 20). FULL-SERIES VERIFICATION (2026-09-16). The Bank's Basel Disclosures index (https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-disclosures) was re-enumerated in full: it carries 27 PDF links, of which 19 are the Pillar 3 editions cited above (the other 8 are four Remuneration Policy Disclosures, plus REP020, a Climate Change statement, an Anti Money Laundering questionnaire and a LIBOR transition FAQ - none of which contains a key metric). All 27 were re-fetched and returned HTTP 200 with Content-Type application/pdf, %PDF magic bytes and a real pdfinfo page count - no 401/403/429/503 and no soft-404 - and the 19 Pillar 3 editions matched their previously-held copies byte-for-byte on size. Page counts, oldest to newest: 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 21, 22, 26, 44, 43, 46, 57, 59, 65, 69, 63, 64, 64. Every URL cited above is therefore a LIVE, verified link; no archive or fallback URL is needed for any of them, and none has been removed.
EVERY EDITION IS DATED FROM ITS OWN COVER PAGE, NOT FROM ITS FILENAME AND NOT FROM THE INDEX LABEL, because for this Bank the three disagree. Two confirmed disagreements: (1) `basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf` - the FILENAME IS WRONG. Its cover reads 'Basel II - Pillar 3 disclosures for the year ended March 31, 2012', and the index label agrees ('for 2011- 2012'); only the filename says 2011. The genuine FY2011 edition is the separate file `basel2_disclosures_FY10_11.pdf`, whose cover reads 'year ended 31 March 2011'. This workbook maps them accordingly. (2) `disclosures2013-14.pdf` - the INDEX LABEL IS WRONG ON REGIME. The index calls it 'Basel II Pillar 3 Disclosures for 2013-2014', but the document's own cover and every page's running head read 'Basel III - Pillar 3 Disclosures / 31 March 2014', and its section 2 describes implementing CRD IV from January 1, 2014. The period (year ended 31 March 2014) is right; the regime label is not. FY2014 is this Bank's FIRST CRD IV year and is treated as such throughout this workbook - it is NOT grouped with the Basel II years. Every other edition's filename, index label and cover agree with one another.
ENTITY RE-CONFIRMED ON ALL 19: each opens "ICICI Bank UK PLC ('the Bank') ... a wholly owned subsidiary of ICICI Bank Limited" and states the disclosures are prepared for ICICI Bank UK PLC, expressly distinguishing itself from the parent's own consolidated disclosures. No ICICI Bank Limited (parent) figure enters any sheet. One hosting-path artefact worth recording so it is not mistaken for a parent-entity document: the FY2024 edition is served from an `/content/dam/icicibank/india/managed-assets/...` path, but the document itself is the UK entity's, verified on its own cover and Overview section.
PRINTED-TOTAL SANITY CHECK ON EVERY DISCLOSED RWA. For each of FY2017-FY2026, that year's own CET1 and total capital divided by that year's own printed total RWA reproduce that year's own printed CET1 and total capital ratios, confirming the printed total is a genuine total and not a credit-risk subtotal mislabelled as one. The single departure is FY2024 (311.3 / 1,546.2 = 20.13% against a printed 20.14%), which is rounding in the one-decimal-place capital figure, not a basis difference. For FY2021-FY2026 the printed total additionally equals the exact sum of its own printed UK OV1 components.
SECOND, NON-PDF SOURCE CHECKED (2026-09-16) - 'Composition of Regulatory Capital' web disclosures. The same Basel Disclosures index also links ten HTML pages (not PDFs, so they are easily missed when enumerating the index's PDF links), each titled 'Disclosure: Composition of Regulatory Capital as at March 31, YYYY' and presented in USD'000. All ten were fetched on 2026-09-16 (HTTP 200, text/html) and read. They corroborate this workbook's capital figures from an independent source: FY2014 Total Tier one capital after deductions 495,095 + 89,759 + 50,000 - 11,061 = 623,794 (workbook 623.8) and Tier one plus tier two after deductions 814,419 (workbook 814.4); FY2015 538,819 (538.8) and 707,047 (707.0); FY2016 537,633 (537.6) and 684,316 (684.3). They contain NO risk-weighted assets, NO risk-weighted exposure amount and NO capital ratio of any kind, so they do not and cannot supply the FY2014-FY2016 total RWA. Note the difference in vintage terminology: these pages use the pre-CRD IV 'Core Tier one / Upper tier two / Lower tier two' captions even for FY2014-FY2016, whereas the corresponding Pillar 3 editions use CRD IV captions for the same amounts; no figure has been mapped across that caption difference beyond the totals above, which are identical either way. FY2008-FY2013 - BASEL II ERA, KEY METRICS NOT DISCLOSED (enumerated, not merely unfound). The Bank published an annual Pillar 3 disclosure for every one of these years and all six were downloaded, cover-date-verified and read in full on 2026-09-15. They are short (10 pages each) BIPRU Chapter 11 / Basel II documents, a different regulatory regime from the CRR / Basel III disclosures used for FY2014 onward, and they contain NO capital resources table, NO Tier 1 / Tier 2 / total capital figure, NO capital ratio, NO total risk-weighted assets, NO leverage ratio, NO LCR and NO NSFR. Their only capital content is a table captioned 'the Bank's Pillar 1 capital requirement by each of the standardised credit risk exposure classes' - credit risk ONLY, with no operational-risk or market-risk capital requirement anywhere in the document. Those printed credit-risk Pillar 1 capital requirement totals are, in USD million: FY2013 241.69, FY2012 260.31, FY2011 402.24, FY2010 459.49, FY2009 426.62, FY2008 335.02. They are recorded here for traceability and are deliberately NOT converted to an RWA figure. Two independent reasons: this workbook does not compute figures at all (as of 2026-09-16 the FY2014-FY2016 Total RWAs, which had been derived that way, were withdrawn - see the Total RWAs sheet's note); and even if it did, these documents disclose no operational-risk and no market-risk capital requirement, so a credit-risk-only conversion would silently understate RWA by the two missing components. No key-metric cell is therefore filled for FY2008-FY2013, and none can be from Pillar 3. Documents (dated from each cover page, NOT from the filename - `basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf` is the year ended March 31, 2012, not 2011): FY2013 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/disclosures2012-13.pdf; FY2012 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf; FY2011 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY10_11.pdf; FY2010 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY09_10.pdf; FY2009 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/Basel_II_p3_Disclosure_Statement.pdf; FY2008 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY2008.pdf. Entity confirmed on each: every one opens "ICICI Bank UK PLC ('the Bank') ... a wholly owned subsidiary of ICICI Bank Limited", states "The disclosures have been prepared for ICICI Bank UK PLC", and expressly distinguishes itself from "the consolidated Basel II - Pillar 3 Disclosures made by ICICI Bank Limited ('the Parent Bank')" - no parent-group figure is present in any of them.

No parent-group figures have been substituted.

UK KM1 row 17 - 12-month average LCR (simple average of month-end observations over the 12 months preceding year end)169.42%190.08%240.20%226.83%226.90%
Point-in-time LCR at 31 March, as stated in each year's own disclosure narrative171.83%216.84%221.17%346.1%245.23%329.5%183.3%225.0%203.9%
Note: TWO DIFFERENT MEASURES, SHOWN SEPARATELY - do not merge. Every edition from FY2022 onward prints both, and they differ materially (FY2022: 226.90% average vs 245.23% point-in-time; FY2023: 226.83% vs 346.1%). The average row is UK KM1 row 17, which each edition's own footnote defines as 'computed using the liquidity balances which are calculated as the simple averages of month end observations over the 12 months preceding the end of the year'. The point-in-time row is the figure each edition states in prose - 'The LCR of the Bank as at March 31, XXXX was ...' (FY2018-FY2021 in the liquidity-risk section, FY2022 onward in the KM1 footnote). FY2018-FY2021 have no average figure at all because the UK KM1 template did not yet apply; they were previously sitting on a single undifferentiated 'Liquidity coverage ratio' row alongside FY2022+ averages, and were moved to the point-in-time row on 2026-09-15. FY2021's 329.5% comes from the FY2021 edition's own liquidity-risk section (the FY2022 edition's footnote independently confirms it as 329.48%); it was previously blank because the FY2022 KM1 leaves the 31 March 2021 comparative empty in rows 15-17. FY2023's 346.1% is that year's own edition's figure; the FY2024 edition restates the same date to 346.08% - each year's own edition is used, per this workbook's standing convention. The Bank has maintained an LCR since October 1, 2015 per its own disclosures, but published no numeric LCR before FY2018, so FY2014-FY2017 are blank rather than estimated; FY2008-FY2013's Basel II disclosures predate the LCR entirely.
FY2014-FY2017 RE-VERIFIED AS AN ENUMERATED ABSENCE (2026-09-16): all four editions were re-read in full and full-text searched for 'LCR' and 'liquidity coverage'. FY2014 and FY2015 contain neither term at all. FY2016 and FY2017 each contain exactly one mention, in identical prose in their liquidity-risk sections - 'Additionally, from October 1, 2015 the Bank maintains Liquidity Coverage Ratio (LCR) as stipulated by the PRA' - with NO number attached, in the narrative or anywhere else in either document. So the requirement demonstrably applied to this Bank from October 2015 while the ratio itself went unpublished until FY2018; the blanks are a confirmed absence of a figure, not an absence of the requirement, and not an unsearched gap.

Its own FY2021 edition has since been located and read, and it reproduces every figure already carried here exactly - CET1 493.9, Tier 1 493.9, Total capital 586.7, Total RWA 2,075.1, CET1/Tier 1 ratio 23.80%, total capital ratio 28.27% (Annexure I transitional own funds template rows 59-63 and LRCom row 20). The same document's 31 March 2020 comparatives (440.9 / 547.2 / 2,941.4 / 14.99% / 18.60%) likewise reproduce this workbook's FY2020 figures exactly. No restatement in either direction. The upgrade additionally supplies FY2021's leverage and LCR figures, which the FY2022 KM1 had left blank - see those sheets' own basis notes.
FULL-SERIES VERIFICATION (2026-09-16). The Bank's Basel Disclosures index (https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-disclosures) was re-enumerated in full: it carries 27 PDF links, of which 19 are the Pillar 3 editions cited above (the other 8 are four Remuneration Policy Disclosures, plus REP020, a Climate Change statement, an Anti Money Laundering questionnaire and a LIBOR transition FAQ - none of which contains a key metric). All 27 were re-fetched and returned HTTP 200 with Content-Type application/pdf, %PDF magic bytes and a real pdfinfo page count - no 401/403/429/503 and no soft-404 - and the 19 Pillar 3 editions matched their previously-held copies byte-for-byte on size. Page counts, oldest to newest: 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 21, 22, 26, 44, 43, 46, 57, 59, 65, 69, 63, 64, 64. Every URL cited above is therefore a LIVE, verified link; no archive or fallback URL is needed for any of them, and none has been removed.
EVERY EDITION IS DATED FROM ITS OWN COVER PAGE, NOT FROM ITS FILENAME AND NOT FROM THE INDEX LABEL, because for this Bank the three disagree. Two confirmed disagreements: (1) `basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf` - the FILENAME IS WRONG. Its cover reads 'Basel II - Pillar 3 disclosures for the year ended March 31, 2012', and the index label agrees ('for 2011- 2012'); only the filename says 2011. The genuine FY2011 edition is the separate file `basel2_disclosures_FY10_11.pdf`, whose cover reads 'year ended 31 March 2011'. This workbook maps them accordingly. (2) `disclosures2013-14.pdf` - the INDEX LABEL IS WRONG ON REGIME. The index calls it 'Basel II Pillar 3 Disclosures for 2013-2014', but the document's own cover and every page's running head read 'Basel III - Pillar 3 Disclosures / 31 March 2014', and its section 2 describes implementing CRD IV from January 1, 2014. The period (year ended 31 March 2014) is right; the regime label is not. FY2014 is this Bank's FIRST CRD IV year and is treated as such throughout this workbook - it is NOT grouped with the Basel II years. Every other edition's filename, index label and cover agree with one another.
ENTITY RE-CONFIRMED ON ALL 19: each opens "ICICI Bank UK PLC ('the Bank') ... a wholly owned subsidiary of ICICI Bank Limited" and states the disclosures are prepared for ICICI Bank UK PLC, expressly distinguishing itself from the parent's own consolidated disclosures. No ICICI Bank Limited (parent) figure enters any sheet. One hosting-path artefact worth recording so it is not mistaken for a parent-entity document: the FY2024 edition is served from an `/content/dam/icicibank/india/managed-assets/...` path, but the document itself is the UK entity's, verified on its own cover and Overview section.
PRINTED-TOTAL SANITY CHECK ON EVERY DISCLOSED RWA. For each of FY2017-FY2026, that year's own CET1 and total capital divided by that year's own printed total RWA reproduce that year's own printed CET1 and total capital ratios, confirming the printed total is a genuine total and not a credit-risk subtotal mislabelled as one. The single departure is FY2024 (311.3 / 1,546.2 = 20.13% against a printed 20.14%), which is rounding in the one-decimal-place capital figure, not a basis difference. For FY2021-FY2026 the printed total additionally equals the exact sum of its own printed UK OV1 components.
SECOND, NON-PDF SOURCE CHECKED (2026-09-16) - 'Composition of Regulatory Capital' web disclosures. The same Basel Disclosures index also links ten HTML pages (not PDFs, so they are easily missed when enumerating the index's PDF links), each titled 'Disclosure: Composition of Regulatory Capital as at March 31, YYYY' and presented in USD'000. All ten were fetched on 2026-09-16 (HTTP 200, text/html) and read. They corroborate this workbook's capital figures from an independent source: FY2014 Total Tier one capital after deductions 495,095 + 89,759 + 50,000 - 11,061 = 623,794 (workbook 623.8) and Tier one plus tier two after deductions 814,419 (workbook 814.4); FY2015 538,819 (538.8) and 707,047 (707.0); FY2016 537,633 (537.6) and 684,316 (684.3). They contain NO risk-weighted assets, NO risk-weighted exposure amount and NO capital ratio of any kind, so they do not and cannot supply the FY2014-FY2016 total RWA. Note the difference in vintage terminology: these pages use the pre-CRD IV 'Core Tier one / Upper tier two / Lower tier two' captions even for FY2014-FY2016, whereas the corresponding Pillar 3 editions use CRD IV captions for the same amounts; no figure has been mapped across that caption difference beyond the totals above, which are identical either way. URLs: FY2019 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-capital-resource-popup-march2019; FY2018 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-capital-resource-popup-march2018; FY2017 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-capital-resource-popup-march2017; FY2016 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-capital-resource-popup-march2016; FY2015 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2015; FY2014 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2014; FY2013 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2013; FY2012 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2012; FY2011 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup_march2011; FY2010 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel_capital_resource_popup.
FY2008-FY2013 - BASEL II ERA, KEY METRICS NOT DISCLOSED (enumerated, not merely unfound). The Bank published an annual Pillar 3 disclosure for every one of these years and all six were downloaded, cover-date-verified and read in full on 2026-09-15. They are short (10 pages each) BIPRU Chapter 11 / Basel II documents, a different regulatory regime from the CRR / Basel III disclosures used for FY2014 onward, and they contain NO capital resources table, NO Tier 1 / Tier 2 / total capital figure, NO capital ratio, NO total risk-weighted assets, NO leverage ratio, NO LCR and NO NSFR. Their only capital content is a table captioned 'the Bank's Pillar 1 capital requirement by each of the standardised credit risk exposure classes' - credit risk ONLY, with no operational-risk or market-risk capital requirement anywhere in the document. Those printed credit-risk Pillar 1 capital requirement totals are, in USD million: FY2013 241.69, FY2012 260.31, FY2011 402.24, FY2010 459.49, FY2009 426.62, FY2008 335.02. They are recorded here for traceability and are deliberately NOT converted to an RWA figure. Two independent reasons: this workbook does not compute figures at all (as of 2026-09-16 the FY2014-FY2016 Total RWAs, which had been derived that way, were withdrawn - see the Total RWAs sheet's note); and even if it did, these documents disclose no operational-risk and no market-risk capital requirement, so a credit-risk-only conversion would silently understate RWA by the two missing components. No key-metric cell is therefore filled for FY2008-FY2013, and none can be from Pillar 3. Documents (dated from each cover page, NOT from the filename - `basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf` is the year ended March 31, 2012, not 2011): FY2013 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/disclosures2012-13.pdf; FY2012 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf; FY2011 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY10_11.pdf; FY2010 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY09_10.pdf; FY2009 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/Basel_II_p3_Disclosure_Statement.pdf; FY2008 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY2008.pdf. Entity confirmed on each: every one opens "ICICI Bank UK PLC ('the Bank') ... a wholly owned subsidiary of ICICI Bank Limited", states "The disclosures have been prepared for ICICI Bank UK PLC", and expressly distinguishes itself from "the consolidated Basel II - Pillar 3 Disclosures made by ICICI Bank Limited ('the Parent Bank')" - no parent-group figure is present in any of them.
ENTITY NOTE: ICICI Bank UK Plc (Companies House 04663024, FRN 223268, LEI 2138002XB6T14IGKGU43) is the UK-incorporated PRA-authorised bank entity. Companies House confirms it is an active public limited company, incorporated 11 February 2003, with accounts filed at Companies House through 31 March 2025; the FY2026 (year ended 31 March 2026) Annual Report and Pillar 3 disclosure are published on the Bank's own website and were used for FY2026, ahead of that year's Companies House filing. The official Basel disclosures identify the reporting entity as ICICI Bank UK PLC and present the UK KM1 data on a standalone Bank basis in USD millions. No parent-group figures have been substituted.

Note: FY2014-FY2021 ARE 'NOT APPLICABLE', NOT MISSING. The PRA introduced the NSFR framework in the UK with effect from 1 January 2022; before that there was no UK NSFR requirement and the Bank published no figure. Its own FY2018-FY2021 editions say it 'tracks its Net Stable Funding ratio (NSFR), though it is yet to be introduced as a regulatory requirement' in the UK. These years are marked explicitly so the gap is not re-chased. FY2008-FY2013's Basel II disclosures predate the concept entirely and are outside this sheet's year range.
BASIS BREAK AT FY2023, NOT AT FY2022. FY2022's KM1 figure of 141.69% is NOT an average: that edition's own note (g) says 'The NSFR ratio in the above table is computed as at March 31, 2022 in line with this regulation', and the FY2023 edition's note (h) confirms 'As the NSFR requirement became applicable from January 1, 2022, the average for the previous year (FY 2022) was not computed'. So 141.69% sits on the point-in-time row and the average row starts at FY2023. From FY2023 each edition's own note defines the KM1 figure as 'the simple averages of four data sets covering the latest and the three previous quarters during the year' and separately states the point-in-time figure ('The NSFR of the Bank as at March 31, XXXX was ...'). The two differ materially - FY2023 147.72% average vs 169.72% spot, FY2026 136.53% vs 132.18% - and must not be merged. The point-in-time row was added 2026-09-15.

Two independent reasons: this workbook does not compute figures at all (as of 2026-09-16 the FY2014-FY2016 Total RWAs, which had been derived that way, were withdrawn - see the Total RWAs sheet's note); and even if it did, these documents disclose no operational-risk and no market-risk capital requirement, so a credit-risk-only conversion would silently understate RWA by the two missing components. No key-metric cell is therefore filled for FY2008-FY2013, and none can be from Pillar 3. Documents (dated from each cover page, NOT from the filename - `basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf` is the year ended March 31, 2012, not 2011): FY2013 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/disclosures2012-13.pdf; FY2012 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf; FY2011 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY10_11.pdf; FY2010 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY09_10.pdf; FY2009 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/Basel_II_p3_Disclosure_Statement.pdf; FY2008 - https://www.icicibank.co.uk/content/dam/icicibank/icici-assets/uk/basel2_disclosures_FY2008.pdf. Entity confirmed on each: every one opens "ICICI Bank UK PLC ('the Bank') ... a wholly owned subsidiary of ICICI Bank Limited", states "The disclosures have been prepared for ICICI Bank UK PLC", and expressly distinguishes itself from "the consolidated Basel II - Pillar 3 Disclosures made by ICICI Bank Limited ('the Parent Bank')" - no parent-group figure is present in any of them.
ENTITY NOTE: ICICI Bank UK Plc (Companies House 04663024, FRN 223268, LEI 2138002XB6T14IGKGU43) is the UK-incorporated PRA-authorised bank entity. Companies House confirms it is an active public limited company, incorporated 11 February 2003, with accounts filed at Companies House through 31 March 2025; the FY2026 (year ended 31 March 2026) Annual Report and Pillar 3 disclosure are published on the Bank's own website and were used for FY2026, ahead of that year's Companies House filing. The official Basel disclosures identify the reporting entity as ICICI Bank UK PLC and present the UK KM1 data on a standalone Bank basis in USD millions. No parent-group figures have been substituted.

ENUMERATED ABSENCE, NOT AN UNSEARCHED GAP (2026-09-16): all 19 Pillar 3 editions the Bank has ever published - FY2008 through FY2026, every one of them downloaded and full-text searched - were searched for 'MREL', 'minimum requirement for own funds and eligible liabilities' and 'loss-absorbing capacity'. There is exactly one hit across the entire 19-document series, and it is not an MREL disclosure: the FY2017 edition's leverage-risk narrative uses the phrase 'loss absorbing capacity in times of a stress' in describing why leverage risk is managed. No MREL requirement, ratio, or eligible-liabilities figure is disclosed in any year. This is consistent with the Bank's size and resolution strategy - the Bank states it is not in scope of the PRA's LREQ leverage regime either - but the point here is only that the documents contain no figure to transcribe.

FY2021 SOURCE UPGRADE (2026-09-15): FY2021 previously relied on the 31 March 2021 comparative column of the FY2022 edition. Its own FY2021 edition has since been located and read, and it reproduces every figure already carried here exactly - CET1 493.9, Tier 1 493.9, Total capital 586.7, Total RWA 2,075.1, CET1/Tier 1 ratio 23.80%, total capital ratio 28.27% (Annexure I transitional own funds template rows 59-63 and LRCom row 20). The same document's 31 March 2020 comparatives (440.9 / 547.2 / 2,941.4 / 14.99% / 18.60%) likewise reproduce this workbook's FY2020 figures exactly. No restatement in either direction. The upgrade additionally supplies FY2021's leverage and LCR figures, which the FY2022 KM1 had left blank - see those sheets' own basis notes.
FULL-SERIES VERIFICATION (2026-09-16). The Bank's Basel Disclosures index (https://www.icicibank.co.uk/personal/basel-disclosures) was re-enumerated in full: it carries 27 PDF links, of which 19 are the Pillar 3 editions cited above (the other 8 are four Remuneration Policy Disclosures, plus REP020, a Climate Change statement, an Anti Money Laundering questionnaire and a LIBOR transition FAQ - none of which contains a key metric). All 27 were re-fetched and returned HTTP 200 with Content-Type application/pdf, %PDF magic bytes and a real pdfinfo page count - no 401/403/429/503 and no soft-404 - and the 19 Pillar 3 editions matched their previously-held copies byte-for-byte on size. Page counts, oldest to newest: 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 21, 22, 26, 44, 43, 46, 57, 59, 65, 69, 63, 64, 64. Every URL cited above is therefore a LIVE, verified link; no archive or fallback URL is needed for any of them, and none has been removed.
EVERY EDITION IS DATED FROM ITS OWN COVER PAGE, NOT FROM ITS FILENAME AND NOT FROM THE INDEX LABEL, because for this Bank the three disagree. Two confirmed disagreements: (1) `basel2_disclosures_March31_11.pdf` - the FILENAME IS WRONG. Its cover reads 'Basel II - Pillar 3 disclosures for the year ended March 31, 2012', and the index label agrees ('for 2011- 2012'); only the filename says 2011. The genuine FY2011 edition is the separate file `basel2_disclosures_FY10_11.pdf`, whose cover reads 'year ended 31 March 2011'. This workbook maps them accordingly. (2) `disclosures2013-14.pdf` - the INDEX LABEL IS WRONG ON REGIME. The index calls it 'Basel II Pillar 3 Disclosures for 2013-2014', but the document's own cover and every page's running head read 'Basel III - Pillar 3 Disclosures / 31 March 2014', and its section 2 describes implementing CRD IV from January 1, 2014. The period (year ended 31 March 2014) is right; the regime label is not. FY2014 is this Bank's FIRST CRD IV year and is treated as such throughout this workbook - it is NOT grouped with the Basel II years. Every other edition's filename, index label and cover agree with one another.
ENTITY RE-CONFIRMED ON ALL 19: each opens "ICICI Bank UK PLC ('the Bank') ... a wholly owned subsidiary of ICICI Bank Limited" and states the disclosures are prepared for ICICI Bank UK PLC, expressly distinguishing itself from the parent's own consolidated disclosures. No ICICI Bank Limited (parent) figure enters any sheet. One hosting-path artefact worth recording so it is not mistaken for a parent-entity document: the FY2024 edition is served from an `/content/dam/icicibank/india/managed-assets/...` path, but the document itself is the UK entity's, verified on its own cover and Overview section.
PRINTED-TOTAL SANITY CHECK ON EVERY DISCLOSED RWA. For each of FY2017-FY2026, that year's own CET1 and total capital divided by that year's own printed total RWA reproduce that year's own printed CET1 and total capital ratios, confirming the printed total is a genuine total and not a credit-risk subtotal mislabelled as one. The single departure is FY2024 (311.3 / 1,546.2 = 20.13% against a printed 20.14%), which is rounding in the one-decimal-place capital figure, not a basis difference. For FY2021-FY2026 the printed total additionally equals the exact sum of its own printed UK OV1 components.
SECOND, NON-PDF SOURCE CHECKED (2026-09-16) - 'Composition of Regulatory Capital' web disclosures. The same Basel Disclosures index also links ten HTML pages (not PDFs, so they are easily missed when enumerating the index's PDF links), each titled 'Disclosure: Composition of Regulatory Capital as at March 31, YYYY' and presented in USD'000. All ten were fetched on 2026-09-16 (HTTP 200, text/html) and read. They corroborate this workbook's capital figures from an independent source: FY2014 Total Tier one capital after deductions 495,095 + 89,759 + 50,000 - 11,061 = 623,794 (workbook 623.8) and Tier one plus tier two after deductions 814,419 (workbook 814.4); FY2015 538,819 (538.8) and 707,047 (707.0); FY2016 537,633 (537.6) and 684,316 (684.3). They contain NO risk-weighted assets, NO risk-weighted exposure amount and NO capital ratio of any kind, so they do not and cannot supply the FY2014-FY2016 total RWA. Note the difference in vintage terminology: these pages use the pre-CRD IV 'Core Tier one / Upper tier two / Lower tier two' captions even for FY2014-FY2016, whereas the corresponding Pillar 3 editions use CRD IV captions for the same amounts; no figure has been mapped across that caption difference beyond the totals above, which are identical either way. FY2008-FY2013 - BASEL II ERA, KEY METRICS NOT DISCLOSED (enumerated, not merely unfound). The Bank published an annual Pillar 3 disclosure for every one of these years and all six were downloaded, cover-date-verified and read in full on 2026-09-15. They are short (10 pages each) BIPRU Chapter 11 / Basel II documents, a different regulatory regime from the CRR / Basel III disclosures used for FY2014 onward, and they contain NO capital resources table, NO Tier 1 / Tier 2 / total capital figure, NO capital ratio, NO total risk-weighted assets, NO leverage ratio, NO LCR and NO NSFR. Their only capital content is a table captioned 'the Bank's Pillar 1 capital requirement by each of the standardised credit risk exposure classes' - credit risk ONLY, with no operational-risk or market-risk capital requirement anywhere in the document. Those printed credit-risk Pillar 1 capital requirement totals are, in USD million: FY2013 241.69, FY2012 260.31, FY2011 402.24, FY2010 459.49, FY2009 426.62, FY2008 335.02. They are recorded here for traceability and are deliberately NOT converted to an RWA figure. Two independent reasons: this workbook does not compute figures at all (as of 2026-09-16 the FY2014-FY2016 Total RWAs, which had been derived that way, were withdrawn - see the Total RWAs sheet's note); and even if it did, these documents disclose no operational-risk and no market-risk capital requirement, so a credit-risk-only conversion would silently understate RWA by the two missing components. No key-metric cell is therefore filled for FY2008-FY2013, and none can be from Pillar 3.
